# Add 62 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Add 62 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Date: 2026-10-18 · Review: ready

Snapshot check: upstream heads were not checked; pinned evidence is a historical snapshot.

### Identity (reviewed)

Maintained native 6502 assembler for SpartaDOS X and BW-DOS, restoring Marek Goderski's historical Fast Assembler and adding parser, arithmetic, diagnostics and memory-management fixes. Reviewed FAMAIN's multi-pass assembly driver and FAPARSE implementation establish substantive tooling.
- https://github.com/HolgerJanz/FastAssembler/blob/c637c1d464c3ffbc4d138521bd05db1a2cb979fd/README.md
- https://github.com/HolgerJanz/FastAssembler/blob/c637c1d464c3ffbc4d138521bd05db1a2cb979fd/1.8/SOURCES/FAMAIN.ICL
- https://github.com/HolgerJanz/FastAssembler/blob/c637c1d464c3ffbc4d138521bd05db1a2cb979fd/1.8/SOURCES/FAPARSE.ICL

- https://github.com/HolgerJanz/FastAssembler/tree/c637c1d464c3ffbc4d138521bd05db1a2cb979fd

### Classification (reviewed)

Pinned implementation, repository tree, build documentation where present, licence evidence and full latest commit message were inspected. No upstream code executed. Latest commit: Add book info README explicitly records original 1.7 public-domain release; FAMAIN embeds 'Public Domain 2021'. No standalone licence; bundled XEDIT attribution/permission does not establish blanket rights for every bundled component.

Original native Atari sliding-tile puzzle from ABBUC 2021: combines colored tiles, with deterministic progression rather than a random generator. Inspected INCMAIN initializes GTIA colors, input and game transitions; the source set includes Atari computer and 5200 entry points.

### Identity (reviewed)

Historical Atari Kermit terminal and reliable-transfer implementation maintained in ca65-compatible source. Its large implementation documents Apple-to-C64-to-Atari ancestry, Atari CIO/850 I/O conversion and decades of transfer and terminal fixes, rather than only shipping binaries.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Atari port of Juan J. Martinez's MSX single-screen platformer, written in WNF and 6502 assembly. Inspected night-knight.wnf implements hardware setup, game state, enemy dispatch, lives and PAL/NTSC timing, with 80 level data files and sprite/runtime modules.

### Identity (reviewed)

Binary-patching and replacement-loader project for C64 Wasteland, moving disk I/O to IDE64 and separating saves, fixing PAL/NTSC timing and turbo-speed behavior. Reviewed wasteland_fix.asm patches fixed-address game routines, requesters and interrupt vectors, and incorporates loader/fileops modules.

### Identity (reviewed)

Client/server space-game proof of concept with a native C64 assembly client and Java server. Reviewed vortex.s initializes DHCP/UDP, login, interrupts, sprites and music; server Universe.java creates the shared map, entities, planetoids and destructible scenery.

### Identity (reviewed)

Dependency-free C disk-image tool implementing classic Macintosh MFS/HFS allocation, catalogs/B-trees, Finder folder metadata, resource forks and MacBinary import/export. It edits actual disk structures rather than wrapping an emulator.

### Identity (reviewed)

Restoration and Retro68 adaptation of Erny Tontlinger's 1992 serial-terminal source distribution, preserving C interpreter, serial I/O, capture/scrolling, MacBinary and X/Y/ZModem/QuickB transfers with THINK C/MPW artifacts.

### Identity (reviewed)

Portable 32/64-bit MDX playback library derived from GORRY's MXDRVg C conversion of disassembled X68000 MXDRV.X and X68Sound chip emulation. Adds isolated contexts, register/key-on APIs and sample WAV/player frontends.
- https://github.com/yosshin4004/portable_mdx/blob/dc50b060405a9e82c3b64573b4e524cd8ba83ff3/readme.md

- https://github.com/yosshin4004/portable_mdx/commit/dc50b060405a9e82c3b64573b4e524cd8ba83ff3

### Classification (reviewed)

Pinned implementation, build instructions and latest full commit message reviewed. Source review establishes project substance, not independent build/runtime validation. Mixed licensing: new Yosshin code Apache-2.0; inherited MXDRV/MXDRVg has no clear formal license and is described as X68-style freeware; X68Sound grants free modification/distribution; SDL2 examples zlib. Do not label entire tree Apache.

### Identity (reviewed)

A native Neo Geo Pocket Color homebrew reinterpretation of Atari's Asteroids. Its own C entity pool implements rotating ship sprites, thrust, asteroid splitting, aimed UFO fire, collisions, waves and flash high scores; it is neither an arcade CPU wrapper nor a recovered Atari binary.
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-asteroids-neo/blob/6a585bb7250d8b91ee7999f83176100049294bda/README.md
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-asteroids-neo/blob/6a585bb7250d8b91ee7999f83176100049294bda/LICENSE

### Classification (reviewed)

Multi-module source and released ROM are present. README roadmap and current source disagree on sprite-plane migration, so implementation is stronger evidence than feature prose. Flash high-score support remains hardware-sensitive. MIT for project code, original framework terms for ameliandev components; proprietary Toshiba compiler excluded. Asteroids name and original game remain third-party IP.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native C adaptation of Konami's 1982 arcade maze shooter for Neo Geo Pocket Color. Separate game, map, screen and entity modules implement keys, treasure, teleport zones, doors and enemy spawn/chase/patrol behavior, rather than emulating the arcade board.
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-tutankham/blob/b0500a7dcb79272d280206e2d27c9e440bfa7920/README.md
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-tutankham/blob/b0500a7dcb79272d280206e2d27c9e440bfa7920/LICENSE
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-tutankham/blob/b0500a7dcb79272d280206e2d27c9e440bfa7920/Makefile
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-tutankham/blob/b0500a7dcb79272d280206e2d27c9e440bfa7920/src/entities.c
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-tutankham/commit/b0500a7dcb79272d280206e2d27c9e440bfa7920
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-tutankham/tree/b0500a7dcb79272d280206e2d27c9e440bfa7920

### Classification (reviewed)

Eight-level/two-zone design is documented and enemy behavior is implemented. Some README sound descriptions are stale; pinned commit changes real sound table constness. README says build/tutankham.ngp while Makefile actually targets bin/tutankham.ngp. MIT file exists but its appended disclaimer is copied from Asteroids and still names Atari/Asteroids. Do not infer rights to Konami game identity/assets from that boilerplate; framework terms and proprietary compiler separately apply.
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-tutankham/blob/b0500a7dcb79272d280206e2d27c9e440bfa7920/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

A native Neo Geo Pocket Color Frogger adaptation with implemented scrolling traffic, logs, diving turtles, homes, timer, fly bonus and pond progression. Source variants support arcade portrait and Coleco-style VFD presentations within one project lineage.
- https://github.com/underscore42/ngpc-frogger-neo/blob/bab2493d8498233c284f7e6a38e8b7b607c5cf21/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantive self-contained gameplay in src/main.c and a separate src-vfd variant. Flash save is explicitly stubbed; high scores are session-only. Three ROM presentations are one candidate, not three projects. MIT covers authored code, but README explicitly credits original Konami arcade sprite rips and restricts fan project to non-commercial distribution. Assets are not made MIT by code licence.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original Neo Geo Pocket Color homebrew inspired by Pengo. C modules implement authored grids, sliding ice blocks, crush rules, hatching and pursuing Sno-Bees, wall stuns, lives and level timing. README explicitly says no original Pengo code, artwork, audio or data is used.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native C Neo Geo Pocket Color adaptation of Q*bert with pyramid tile states, Coily pursuit, falling balls, rescue discs, rotated D-pad movement and high-score entry. It packages its own NGPC ROM rather than running an arcade emulator.

README identifies Sony Pictures trademark and non-commercial tribute, so asset rights are separate.

### Identity (reviewed)

A substantial Eclipse Theia/Electron IDE dedicated to VUEngine and Nintendo Virtual Boy development. Custom TypeScript/React extension implements project-aware code generation, hardware-specific editors and build integration, including a layer/frame pixel editor with tile grids. Distinct development tool from the already tracked VUEngine runtime.

### Identity (reviewed)

Linux C host utility implementing Sega Saturn USB DataLink packet/checksum and FTDI transport logic. It can dump the BIOS, read/write Saturn memory and upload/execute homebrew, enabling native console development and inspection without the original Windows-only software.
- https://github.com/VGKintsugi/Satlink-Linux/blob/38cb69affa412241dcb85cb1ec1d72b044a6901f/README.md
- https://github.com/VGKintsugi/Satlink-Linux/blob/38cb69affa412241dcb85cb1ec1d72b044a6901f/LICENSE

- https://github.com/VGKintsugi/Satlink-Linux/tree/38cb69affa412241dcb85cb1ec1d72b044a6901f

### Classification (reviewed)

Transport implementation and CLI source accompany minimal Makefile; external USB DataLink hardware is required. Repository includes a prebuilt executable that was not run. MIT, copyright 2019 VGKintsugi. Referenced external DataLink protocol specification and hardware have independent terms.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable Sega Saturn emulator for Nintendo Wii derived from Yabause Wii and selected Yaba Sanshiro improvements. Substantial fork includes Hollywood GPU rendering and its own SH-2-to-PowerPC dynamic recompiler, with dual SH-2 machine state, rather than being a game-specific emulation wrapper.
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/README.md
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/LICENSE
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/Makefile
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/src/sh2/sh2.c
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/src/sh2/drc/compiler.c
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/commit/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/tree/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26

### Classification (reviewed)

Pinned source contains real dual-SH2 reset/on-chip state and opcode metadata/JIT compiler implementation. README explicitly calls it experimental with bugs, crashes and softlocks. User must supply their own Saturn BIOS and game images. GPL-2.0 project and compiler source headers; bundled third-party libraries have their own licences. BIOS and commercial games are not supplied or licensed by the emulator.
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/README.md
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/LICENSE
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/Makefile
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/src/sh2/sh2.c
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/src/sh2/drc/compiler.c
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/commit/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/tree/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Guest Saturn main CPUs are dual SH-2; included Musashi core emulates the 68000 sound CPU. These are guest input architectures, not devkitPPC host architecture.
- https://github.com/fadedled/seta-gx/blob/facc5fca0f9eb5e456ca8b2c4289cf56523b9d26/README.md

### Target CPU (reviewed)

Wii Broadway/Gekko-family PowerPC target; compiler.c explicitly emits PPC instructions from SH-2 guest code.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Sega Saturn C 3D platformer/demo built on Jo Engine and SGL. A substantial main.c implements player movement/collision, enemies, powerups, title and level selection, camera modes, object viewing and an interactive map builder, with Saturn texture/map assets and Jo entry point.

Roughly 153KB main implementation plus collision/object/player headers and game assets. Has uploaded ISO/ELF/binaries but none were executed. No README; some unused/sample code and inherited headers make project maturity uncertain. main.c retains Jo Engine's BSD-3-Clause-style header. No root licence or explicit project-wide grant was found; cd/CPY.TXT is empty. Do not assume all project assets/added code share the inherited sample header.

### Identity (reviewed)

Cross-platform Go command-line tool for constructing Sega Saturn homebrew CUE sheets from an ISO and numbered audio track files. Actual implementation locates tracks, identifies WAVE/BINARY formats, sorts by track number, formats pregaps and writes the cue file.
- https://github.com/DarkD04/saturncue/blob/ec9225f48dc84d2128e602f5a079ff0b2967b622/README.md
- https://github.com/DarkD04/saturncue/blob/ec9225f48dc84d2128e602f5a079ff0b2967b622/Makefile
- https://github.com/DarkD04/saturncue/blob/ec9225f48dc84d2128e602f5a079ff0b2967b622/main.go
- https://github.com/DarkD04/saturncue/commit/ec9225f48dc84d2128e602f5a079ff0b2967b622
- https://github.com/DarkD04/saturncue/tree/ec9225f48dc84d2128e602f5a079ff0b2967b622

### Classification (reviewed)

Small but complete implementation, not a placeholder. README says pregap defaults to zero, while source initializes it to one second and prints a conflicting zero-default message; do not repeat README default uncritically. Track regexp accepts only a single digit after optional zero. No licence file or explicit licence notice found in pinned tree/source; public availability alone does not grant redistribution rights.

### Identity (reviewed)

Standalone C++ disassembler for 16-bit DOS MZ and COM images. It uses Capstone for instruction decoding and implements its own binary loading and experimental recursive traversal and cross-reference discovery.

Implementation inspected: CS_MODE_16 initialization and branch operand extraction in disassembler.cpp; segment-aware far-call handling and address graph in analyzer.cpp. Recursive traversal explicitly experimental. GPL-2.0 licence text present; no stronger version-or-later claim inferred.

### Identity (reviewed)

C# disassembler and analyst for 16-bit NE binaries, originally designed for MajorBBS and Worldgroup modules but also supporting general Win16 EXE/DLL files. Enhanced analysis resolves imports, relocation references, strings and compiler idioms.
- https://github.com/mbbsemu/MBBSDASM/blob/52dc12ada40f6e7603da3adf4aefc2a598306a78/readme.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Disassembler.cs walks NE segments, disassembles code and applies relocation/import/string/jump/call resolution. Tests and MajorBBS/GALGSBL/DOSCALLS definition databases included. README lists MZ support as future work, so it is not claimed. BSD-2-Clause; dependencies carry their own licences.

### Identity (reviewed)

C++ libopcodes-based executable disassembler derived from swdisasm. It parses DOS MZ/LE/LX linear executable structures and emits AT&T/GAS-style assembly intended to support analysis and reassembly.
- https://github.com/samunders-core/le_disasm/blob/8052a7e1b7d883aa5120e58805b984093935e669/README.md
- https://github.com/samunders-core/le_disasm/blob/8052a7e1b7d883aa5120e58805b984093935e669/le/lin_ex.h
- https://github.com/samunders-core/le_disasm/blob/8052a7e1b7d883aa5120e58805b984093935e669/main.cpp
- https://github.com/samunders-core/le_disasm/blob/8052a7e1b7d883aa5120e58805b984093935e669/LICENSE
- https://github.com/samunders-core/le_disasm/commit/8052a7e1b7d883aa5120e58805b984093935e669

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantive executable-image parser, fixup, region and instruction infrastructure present. Historical upstream assembly success is a claim, not independently verified. OS/2 is format coverage, not a verified OS/2 application runtime. GPL-3.0 licence text present.

### Identity (reviewed)

Alone in the Dark-family research tools: LIFE/TRACK script disassemblers, PAK extraction/repacking and background export, live DOSBox memory/variable views, and recorded-session playback for inspection.
- https://github.com/tigrouind/AITD-tools/blob/17f4d32c4890f28904b3631e6fe1767b581557be/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Wine-derived compatibility layer that runs Windows 1.x–3.x executables on 64-bit Windows using CPU emulation, Win16-to-Win32 API conversion, DOS services and handle translation.
- https://github.com/otya128/winevdm/blob/a31d1c27d474a62823b2cec404d10c3888e6ee80/README.md

- https://github.com/otya128/winevdm/commit/a31d1c27d474a62823b2cec404d10c3888e6ee80

### Classification (reviewed)

otvdm/winevdm.c implements executable launching, path/runtime setup and Wine VDM integration; module tree contains reusable API implementations and CPU cores. DOS support is explicitly incomplete. Root LICENSE contains GPL-2.0 text; inspected Wine loader states LGPL-2.1-or-later. Mixed component licensing, not a uniform root-only attribution.

### Identity (reviewed)

Open-source release of Topten Software Windows 3.0 emulator. Sharp86 CPU emulation loads Win16 applications while implemented APIs are mapped to modern Windows calls; includes debugging and NE module/heap management.

Machine.cs derives from CPU, sets memory bus, heaps, thunks and API modules. Missing APIs raise exceptions; author describes project as handed to community rather than complete Windows compatibility. GPL-3.0-or-later explicitly in source/README and LICENSE.txt.

### Identity (reviewed)

C and NASM reconstruction of the 1987 DOS BARD.EXE, with reconstructed root and overlay modules and a reproducibility-focused Microsoft C 4.0/LINK 3.51 build/compare pipeline.

M0000.C contains reconstructed character/inventory/stat logic. README claims complete byte-identical 114528-byte EXE including three overlays; comparison pipeline inspected, no independent verification. No explicit licence file/grant found. Reconstructed proprietary source rights unclear; original binaries/toolchain excluded.

### Identity (reviewed)

A reusable Rust implementation of the InfoAge UNIVAC 1219 machine and its programming tools. The shared crate implements 18-bit register/memory execution, instructions, arithmetic, I/O and tape handling, with CLI assembler/emulator/disassembler frontends and a browser UI. It is a substantive implementation inspired by Duane Craps’s earlier tools, rather than a packaged museum image.
- https://github.com/TheScienceElf/UNIVAC-1219/blob/93cd39864b9d7acad80885f150df9186cbdc9950/README.md
- https://github.com/TheScienceElf/UNIVAC-1219/blob/93cd39864b9d7acad80885f150df9186cbdc9950/common/src/emulator.rs
- https://github.com/TheScienceElf/UNIVAC-1219/blob/93cd39864b9d7acad80885f150df9186cbdc9950/common/src/assembler.rs

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual 18-bit CPU and assembler code inspected. Upstream explicitly lists unimplemented instructions/flags, approximate emulation and an experimental heuristic disassembler; no complete-machine accuracy claim is made. MIT LICENSE inspected; bundled historic memory/tape examples should not automatically be assumed covered beyond their own provenance.

### Identity (reviewed)

A native UNIVAC 1219 assembly implementation of a 32-bit RISC-V interpreter, with OCaml decoding/code-generation/fuzzing tools and a C build environment. This lets modern C programs compile to RISC-V instructions and execute through an interpreter on the historical 18-bit machine. Its opcode dispatch and arithmetic/memory/branch handlers are present as UNIVAC assembly sources.
- https://github.com/nathanfarlow/univac-1219-riscv/blob/e31e6dff8cf9781598d7ff80c2c5d465f5a77cb9/README.md

### Build (reviewed)

Documented Docker build installs the C/RISC-V and OCaml tooling. Make concatenates/preprocesses .univac sources, assembles a .76 tape and optionally runs the UNIVAC emulator submodule. ### Identity (reviewed)

The actual K&R C Lilith emulator Andreas Borchert developed in 1983 to bootstrap ETH Modula-2 onto a Perkin-Elmer 3220 running Unix VII. The repository preserves the execution engine, loader, storage, Unix service bridge and tracing code. This is historical development software preservation, with known period-compiler dependencies, rather than a new browser emulator.
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-emulator/blob/e1e7820edb514f56ea094f7a8df1491161cea980/README.md
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-emulator/blob/e1e7820edb514f56ea094f7a8df1491161cea980/src/l_exec.c
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-emulator/blob/e1e7820edb514f56ea094f7a8df1491161cea980/src/l_load.c

- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-emulator/commit/e1e7820edb514f56ea094f7a8df1491161cea980

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserved ETH Zürich C18 Modula-2 multipass compiler sources, derived from the 1981 compiler and adapted at Ulm in 1983 for a Lilith emulator under Unix VII. The repository contains actual compiler passes, symbols, code-generation modules, build recipes and steady-state C18 binaries, so it is a source-preservation/toolchain record separate from the emulator that runs it.
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-multipass-modula2-compiler/blob/200c079b9df1fb7c7a874a0da89c94161ddf188b/README.md
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-multipass-modula2-compiler/blob/200c079b9df1fb7c7a874a0da89c94161ddf188b/src/MCPass4.m2
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-multipass-modula2-compiler/blob/200c079b9df1fb7c7a874a0da89c94161ddf188b/src/MCP4CodeSys.m2
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-multipass-modula2-compiler/blob/200c079b9df1fb7c7a874a0da89c94161ddf188b/src/Makefile
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-multipass-modula2-compiler/blob/200c079b9df1fb7c7a874a0da89c94161ddf188b/LICENSE
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-multipass-modula2-compiler/blob/200c079b9df1fb7c7a874a0da89c94161ddf188b/License-Agreement
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-multipass-modula2-compiler/commit/200c079b9df1fb7c7a874a0da89c94161ddf188b

### Classification (reviewed)

These are adapted sources, not an unchanged original ETH release. Actual pass-4 code generation and compiler modules inspected. Period dependencies and source provenance are explicit. Non-free historical ETH licence. LICENSE says redistribution is by sublicense under the attached 1983 agreement, binding recipients to the same terms; do not label GPL or unrestricted open source.
- https://github.com/afborchert/lilith-multipass-modula2-compiler/blob/200c079b9df1fb7c7a874a0da89c94161ddf188b/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

A reconstruction of the 1988 Norsk Data ND-120 3202D CPU board from original design documents, first represented in Logisim and now maintained as Verilog. Includes CPU/decode gate arrays, PAL translations, microcode, MMU and bus devices, simulation harnesses and FPGA board integrations. This is a hardware/firmware reconstruction distinct from the catalogued nd100x and nd500x software emulators.
- https://github.com/RetroCoreLabs/nd-120/blob/f640113d1db7262b78cc5c8e54dc461a311595a7/README.md
- https://github.com/RetroCoreLabs/nd-120/blob/f640113d1db7262b78cc5c8e54dc461a311595a7/Verilog/ND120_CORE.v

README claims SINTRAN III boots on Tang Nano, Nexys and MiSTer; MEGA65 bitstreams are timing-clean but not yet run on real MEGA65. Treat hardware claims as upstream reports. Root MIT licence inspected. Original 1988 design scans, firmware and other inherited material require their own provenance assessment.

### Identity (reviewed)

A C# TMS9900 instruction-level development simulator with a Forth-like interactive monitor, assembler, memory access, single stepping and status/workspace-register state. Source implements the architecture’s RAM-backed register workspace, context switching, flags and instruction dispatch, with tests and usable assembly examples; it deliberately models the CPU rather than recreating a complete TI 990 system.
- https://github.com/rsbohn/tawny-petrel/blob/3364d89766968828f5ec69e6a6e0d61b27d4ba40/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

A source-grounded new reconstruction of NASA MSC’s lost VIEW visualization program from its surviving reports, plots and film output. Fixed-form period-style Fortran implements trajectory, projection, spacecraft and celestial line drawing; a modern shell compiles it for live browser presentation. The project explicitly labels conjectures, modern substitutions and restomod additions rather than claiming recovered original source or an exact UNIVAC executable.
- https://github.com/aaronsb/view1108/blob/6f9ec8289919795590f601b6897b91787a82aa4a/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual Fortran driver and graphics kernels inspected. Approximate ephemerides and modern algorithms are labelled. No surviving original source is claimed; real UNIVAC operation would require changes and timing estimates are unmeasured. MIT code; THIRD_PARTY details BSD/OFL and public-domain data plus rights-unconfirmed film stills and historically copyrighted reference manuals. Root MIT does not blanket all media.

### Identity (reviewed)

IBM’s Python implementation of 3270 terminal interaction, including a TN3270 data-stream engine, zti terminal UI and ati automation compatibility layer. The code implements terminal orders, screen state, EBCDIC handling, session management and protocol negotiation, providing reusable access to historical mainframe interfaces rather than merely launching another emulator.
- https://github.com/IBM/tnz/blob/df9325f1f9706f532464b283effdf8e1a2d62f66/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

A static JavaScript implementation of IBM 3270 and 5250 terminal protocols for mainframe and AS/400/IBM i access. Browser modules implement datastream parsing, fields, screen state, TN3270E/TN5250E negotiation and ENPTUI behavior; connection uses an external TCP-to-WebSocket bridge. Substantive parsers and protocol tests distinguish it from a skin around an existing emulator.
- https://github.com/bencz/IronTerm/blob/f2108b090b9900276eb71d1ad4bd941300aa50cc/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserved and maintained PilRC source for compiling Palm resource scripts into application resource data, including bitmap/font processing and optional preview UI. The implementation includes the real resource parser/serializer, language/type tables, bitmap encoders and font tools, with Palm examples. This is the long-running original Palm tool lineage, not another Palm SDK mirror.

### Identity (reviewed)

The historical pilot-link C libraries and command-line conduits for communicating with Palm handhelds from Unix-like systems. Implements Palm DLP and lower-level link/socket protocols plus record packers, synchronizers and install/backup/debug tools. This is an independent original protocol/tooling lineage, not the already catalogued modern JavaScript palm-sync or palm-pdb projects.
- https://github.com/jichu4n/pilot-link/blob/adcebee10291069b75e2b9b954254eca55e143b5/README
- https://github.com/jichu4n/pilot-link/blob/adcebee10291069b75e2b9b954254eca55e143b5/libpisock/dlp.c

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual DLP request handling, record/database and utility source inspected. Historical release source with maintenance patches; compatibility varies by Palm device/transport. No device was connected. Mixed GNU GPL utilities and GNU Library/Lesser GPL libraries; source headers state Library GPL 2-or-later for libpisock. Do not flatten licensing to a single permissive grant.

## Decisions

```json
[
```
